# Kanban — piano di consolidamento strutturale

> Audit 2026-08-31. 38 agent, 14 dimensioni + 4 gap investigation, verifica avversariale per dimensione.
> **185 finding verificati**: 7 critical, 42 high, 83 medium, 53 low. 147 effort S, 38 M.
> Run workflow: `wf_dd5804b2-3ee`.

## 0. Perché il Kanban sembra "pieno di bug piccoli"

Non sono bug piccoli scorrelati. Sono **7 cause strutturali** che ne generano 185.

| # | Tema | Causa radice | Fix radice |
|---|------|--------------|------------|
| T1 | **L'autorizzazione valida il path, non il body** | `kanbanPermissions.ts` risolve la board dall'id in URL e si ferma lì. Nessuna route/service verifica che gli id nel **body** appartengano a quella board. Il pattern corretto esiste ma per caso: `executeBulkArchive` usa `column: { boardId }` a 280 righe da `moveCard` che non lo fa. | Un `assertBelongsToBoard(boardId, {columnIds, cardIds, userIds})` in `kanbanPermissions.ts`, chiamato nei 6 service che accettano id dal body. E scoping sulle write: `updateMany({where:{id, boardId}})` invece di `update({where:{id}})`. |
| T2 | **`position` è aritmetica incrementale con 4 scrittori che non si parlano** | Il server fa insert-and-shift in 3 `updateMany` dove il terzo usa la posizione **pre-move** su righe già shiftate; il client scrive solo la riga spostata; le card archiviate occupano posizioni invisibili al drop index; il menu manda `999`. Nessun vincolo di unicità, nessun tiebreaker. | Sostituire i 3 `updateMany` con un **resequence** dentro la transazione esistente + estrarre `computeColumnOrder(ids, cardId, newIndex)` puro e testabile. Un cambio, 7 finding. |
| T3 | **SSE è un blast di stringhe senza filtro né ri-autorizzazione** | `broadcast()` scrive lo stesso payload su ogni socket: niente esclusione dell'attore, niente check permessi per connessione (pur avendo `conn.user.id`), autorizzazione solo al connect. Emissione per call-site → `board.service.ts` non importa nemmeno `broadcast`. | Rendere `broadcast()` il chokepoint: `actorId` nell'evento, strip di `card.note` prima di serializzare, `disconnectUser(boardId, userId)` (~8 righe) chiamato dal revoke, e importare `broadcast` in `board.service.ts`. Una funzione + un import = 8 finding. |
| T4 | **Le scritture vanno su Dexie, le letture dal server, e non si riconciliano mai** | Ogni mutation risolve sulla write Dexie (`kanbanService.ts:372-385` non ha HTTP); la board renderizza solo da GET server (`useKanbanBoard.ts:10-11`, nessun fallback Dexie). `flushSync` è fire-and-forget → l'invalidate corre contro la PUT che ha appena accodato. E syncPush riscrive gli id in Dexie dopo un board CREATE **ma non nei payload della coda**. | Tre one-liner, non un rewrite: risolvere `columnId` da Dexie al push; far ritornare a `syncPush` la promise in volo; scopare `useKanbanBoards` per userId. |
| T5 | **La feature non ha superficie d'errore** | `useKanbanMutations`: **`grep -c onError` = 0** su ~20 mutation. `queryClient.ts` non ha `MutationCache`. `api.ts` gestisce solo il 401. Vari call site ci mettono sopra `.catch(() => {})`. Nel frattempo il backend lancia chiavi i18n precise — 3 delle quali non esistono nei locale. | `mutationCache: new MutationCache({ onError: e => toast.error(getApiErrorMessage(e, 'common.genericError')) })` in `lib/queryClient.ts`. `getApiErrorMessage` esiste già (`utils/errorUtils.ts:8`). Un oggetto, e tutte le mutation silenziose iniziano a parlare — in tutta l'app, non solo kanban. |
| T6 | **`getBoard` è una write, ed è anche la fetch per-board del loop di sync** | `board.service.ts:143-160` esegue `archiveCompletedCards` + un auto-heal di `isCompleted` **ad ogni GET**, su una route che richiede solo READ. `syncPull` la chiama per ogni board visibile ogni 30s senza gate su `updatedAt`. Un poll di background muta il DB una volta per board per ciclo. | Togliere le write dalla read (il default `isCompleted` è già in `createBoard`), spostare `archiveCompletedCards` sul cron reminder, poi il gate su `updatedAt` diventa un'ottimizzazione sicura. |
| T7 | **Il binding card↔taskItem viene creato ma mai posseduto, azzerato o invertito** | Il link scrive `kanbanCard.taskItemId`, `moveCard` lo legge per spuntare il TaskItem, ma nessuno possiede il ciclo di vita: l'unlink pulisce solo le colonne, 3 delle 4 operazioni su TaskItem non hanno lato kanban, e l'autorizzazione all'unlink è "chi ha creato il link" senza scappatoia per l'owner. | `unlinkTaskListFromBoard` deve azzerare `taskItemId` sulle card (`updateMany({where:{column:{boardId}}, data:{taskItemId:null}})`), e `board.ownerId` va accettato come secondo unlinker autorizzato. |

## 1. Task list

Ordinata per dipendenza, non per severità. Ogni stage è eseguibile in blocco; dentro Stage 2 e Stage 3 l'ordine è vincolante.

### Stage 0 — Diagnostica (sblocca tutto, nessun file TIER 1)

*Senza questo non puoi validare nessuna delle fix successive: oggi falliscono in silenzio.*

- [ ] **0.1** `MutationCache` con `onError` globale → `frontend/src/lib/queryClient.ts`. Riusa `getApiErrorMessage` (`utils/errorUtils.ts:8`). **S**
- [ ] **0.2** Aggiungere le 3 chiavi i18n mancanti in `en.json` **e** `it.json`: `errors.kanban.ownerOnly`, `kanban.noteLink.sharingGapDescription` (oggi renderizza la chiave grezza come paragrafo esplicativo del modal), `notifications.kanbanBulkMove` (+ `_TITLE` in `backend/src/utils/notificationI18n.ts`). **S**
- [ ] **0.3** Correggere i nomi degli arg nelle notification push: `comments-chat.service.ts:79/135/235` — `commenterName`→`authorName`, `authorName`→`senderName`. Oggi le push arrivano con `{{authorName}}` letterale. **S**
- [ ] **0.4** SSE reconnect su risposta non-OK e su EOF pulito → `useKanbanRealtime.ts:91-99`. Avvolgere in `try/finally` con retry, togliere il `return` nudo. **Vincolo d'ordine: deve stare prima dello Stage 5**, perché il poll chat da 3s è oggi l'unica cosa che tiene viva la chat quando lo stream muore. **S**

### Stage 1 — Buchi di permessi (backend-only, tutti paralleli, nessun TIER 1)

- [ ] **1.1** `assertBelongsToBoard(boardId, {columnIds?, cardIds?, userIds?})` in `backend/src/services/kanbanPermissions.ts`. **S**
- [ ] **1.2** 🔴 **CRITICAL** `moveCard`: `if (targetColumn.boardId !== card.column.boardId) throw ...` → `card.service.ts:198`. I due boardId sono già in mano. *Oggi: un utente con share READ su B enumera le column id di B dal GET board, poi sposta una card della propria board A dentro B, rinumerando la colonna vittima. B non riceve nemmeno l'evento SSE (va alla board sorgente).* **S**
- [ ] **1.3** `reorderColumns`: `updateMany({where:{id, boardId}})` → `column.service.ts:58-65`. Oggi scrive per id nudo, `boardId` è un parametro usato solo per il broadcast. **S**
- [ ] **1.4** Validare `assigneeId` contro i membri della board → `card.service.ts:69` / `routes/kanban.ts:50`. **M**
- [ ] **1.5** Togliere `noteId` da `updateCardSchema` (`routes/kanban.ts:53`) e da `updateCard` (`card.service.ts:70`). Oggi bypassa il check owner di `linking.service` e lascia `noteLinkedById` null → **link non rimuovibile da nessuno**. Zero impatto FE: il link ha già la sua route. **S**
- [ ] **1.6** 🔴 **CRITICAL** `linkTaskListToBoard`: check di accesso alla task list → `linking.service.ts:376`. Oggi accetta qualsiasi `taskListId` e poi **scrive sui TaskItem della vittima**. Il check esiste già in `createBoardFromTaskList`, va copiato. **S**
- [ ] **1.7** `shareWithUserIds` da grant list a filtro: intersecare server-side con i partecipanti della board → `linking.service.ts:99, 208`. **S**
- [ ] **1.8** `checkNoteSharingForBoard`: check owner → `linking.service.ts:19-23`. Oggi ritorna titolo e owner di **qualsiasi** note id. **S**
- [ ] **1.9** `deleteComment`: ri-validare l'accesso alla board → `comments-chat.service.ts:96` / `routes/kanban.ts:516`. **S**
- [ ] **1.10** `shareKanbanBoard`: non retrocedere un ACCEPTED a PENDING sul re-share → `sharing.service.ts:576`. Oggi ri-condividere una board **fa perdere l'accesso** a chi aveva già accettato. **S**

### Stage 2 — Riscrittura di `position` (sequenziale: test prima, backend poi, frontend infine)

*L'ordine è vincolante: la suite attuale passa **con il bug dentro** perché `prismaMock.kanbanCard.updateMany` è stubbato a `{count:0}` e si asserisce solo che `$transaction` sia stata chiamata. Nessun test usa `toColumnId === card.columnId`.*

- [ ] **2.1** Estrarre `computeColumnOrder(cardIds, cardId, newIndex)` puro + table test. **Fallisce oggi.** **M**
- [ ] **2.2** 🟠 Sostituire i 3 `updateMany` di `moveCard` con un resequence su `archivedAt: null` → `card.service.ts:202-232`. *Bug dimostrato: card a 0,1,2,3, sposta la prima in posizione 2 → risultato `A=2, B=0, C=2, D=3`: **due card a 2 e un buco a 1**. È il gesto più comune del kanban.* **M**
- [ ] **2.3** Tiebreaker `orderBy: [{position:'asc'},{createdAt:'asc'}]` → `board.service.ts:172`. **S**
- [ ] **2.4** Eliminare il sentinel `999`: mandare `targetColumn.cards.length` → `useBoardDnD.ts:295`, `KanbanBoardPage.tsx:363`. Oggi `999` viene salvato letteralmente e inverte l'ordine di spostamenti successivi. **S**
- [ ] **2.5** Replicare insert-and-shift nella write Dexie → `kanbanService.ts:376`. **M**
- [ ] **2.6** `createCard` / `createColumn`: aggregate+create dentro `$transaction`. **S**

### Stage 3 — Sync e offline (⚠️ TIER 1: un cambio per commit, verificato da solo)

*Ordinato per raggio d'esplosione. Il primo non tocca nessun file TIER 1 e da solo chiude 6 finding.*

- [ ] **3.1** Scopare `useKanbanBoards` per user id (`useKanbanBoards.ts:7`) + `viewerId` sulle righe shared nei 2 punti di write del pull. *Oggi è un table scan senza scope: dopo un cambio account **le board del profilo precedente vengono renderizzate**, e `SharedUsersModal` ne espone le email dei collaboratori senza round-trip al server.* **S**
- [ ] **3.2** 🔴 **CRITICAL** Risolvere `columnId` da Dexie al push: `(await db.kanbanCards.get(item.entityId))?.columnId ?? item.data.columnId` → `syncService.ts:686`. *Oggi le card create su una board il cui CREATE non è ancora stato pushato puntano a column id locali morti → 404 → **cancellate dalla coda con un console.warn**.* **S**
- [ ] **3.3** Restringere il drop silenzioso su 404 a `item.type !== 'CREATE'` → `syncService.ts:780`. **S**
- [ ] **3.4** `syncPush` deve ritornare la promise in volo, poi `flushSync().then(invalidateBoard)` → `syncService.ts:551`, `useKanbanMutations.ts:17`. *Attenzione: oggi `await syncPush()` è già inutile perché la funzione si auto-defer e ritorna subito.* **M**
- [ ] **3.5** Eliminare la guard "non pushare board shared" → `syncService.ts:632-638`. Oggi **butta via il rename di un collaboratore WRITE**. Il server è già l'autorità (403 → item `failed` già gestito a `:797-806`). **S**
- [ ] **3.6** Eliminare il blocco di pull shared ridondante (`syncService.ts:423-488`) **e nello stesso commit** rilassare il filtro di ownership a `:318-321`. *Quel blocco è l'unico path che pruna le board shared revocate: toglierlo da solo le lascia in Dexie per sempre.* **M**
- [ ] **3.7** try/catch interno separato per notebooks/tags/notes in `syncPull`. Oggi se uno lancia, il prune kanban non gira mai. **S**

### Stage 4 — Chokepoint SSE (dopo lo Stage 1)

- [ ] **4.1** `actorId` in `KanbanEvent` + strip di `card.note` dentro `broadcast()` → `kanbanSSE.ts:102`. *Oggi il titolo della nota linkata viene spinto ai membri che `getBoard` filtra apposta (`helpers.ts:46` mette `note.title` dentro `cardWithAssigneeSelect`).* **S**
- [ ] **4.2** Filtro self-echo lato client in `handleEvent` → `useKanbanRealtime.ts`. Oggi ogni attore riceve il proprio evento: un GET board completo in più per azione + pulse di self-highlight. **S**
- [ ] **4.3** `disconnectUser(boardId, userId)` in `kanbanSSE.ts` (~8 righe, l'handler `res.on('close')` fa già la pulizia), chiamato da `revokeKanbanBoardShare` (`sharing.service.ts:633`). *Oggi al collaboratore revocato **continuano ad arrivare card, commenti e chat** finché tiene il tab aperto.* **S**
- [ ] **4.4** Importare `broadcast` in `board.service.ts` ed emettere `board:updated` da `updateBoard`, `deleteBoard` e le 4 route cover/avatar. *Il tipo evento e l'handler client esistono già entrambi.* **S**
- [ ] **4.5** Invalidare la board query sull'evento `connected` → `useKanbanRealtime.ts:35`. Oggi è un no-op: tutto ciò che succede durante una disconnessione è perso per sempre. **S**
- [ ] **4.6** Allineare l'union frontend: `card:unarchived` e `board:updated` mancano in `types.ts:170`. **S**

### Stage 5 — Carico (dopo la 0.4)

- [ ] **5.1** Togliere `archiveCompletedCards` e l'auto-heal `isCompleted` da `getBoard` → `board.service.ts:143-160`. *Effetto visibile oggi: disattivare "completed" su una colonna si ri-attiva da solo, perché l'invalidate della mutation rifetcha attraverso la stessa write.* **S**
- [ ] **5.2** Gate della fetch di dettaglio in `syncPull` su `board.updatedAt` → `syncService.ts:341`. *Oggi: 20 board = 21 richieste e ~300 statement SQL ogni 30 secondi.* **M**
- [ ] **5.3** Togliere `refetchInterval: 3000` → `useKanbanChat.ts:12`. **S**
- [ ] **5.4** Paginazione chat/commenti: `orderBy: desc` + `.reverse()` → `comments-chat.service.ts:24, 158`. *Oggi ritornano i **50 più vecchi**: oltre i 50 messaggi la chat è congelata e il messaggio appena inviato sparisce dopo l'invalidate.* **S**
- [ ] **5.5** Batch del lookup destinatari + togliere l'`await` da `notifyBoardUsersTiered` → `notifications.ts:99`, `card.service.ts:280`. Oggi: 3 findUnique per destinatario + invio SMTP awaited dentro la request. **S**
- [ ] **5.6** Rimuovere il loop PUT silenzioso duplicato in `handleBulkMove` → `KanbanBoardPage.tsx:360-365`. Oggi ogni card del bulk move viene inviata **due volte**. **S**

### Rinviabile (reale ma non bloccante)

- [ ] **D.1** Batch a11y: 13 bottoni icon-only senza nome accessibile, card apribili da tastiera, submenu hover-only del context menu, hit area del drag handle (4px). Meglio come sweep unico. **M**
- [ ] **D.2** Avviso "filtro attivo = sola lettura" + persistenza filtri. *Oggi digitare **un carattere** nella ricerca rende l'intera board read-only senza spiegazione (`KanbanBoardPage.tsx:878/920`).* **S**
- [ ] **D.3** Cleanup dei file cover/avatar alla cancellazione board + estensione derivata dal mimetype validato (oggi arriva dal filename client, nessuna allowlist → `.svg` servito come documento attivo same-origin non autenticato). **S**
- [ ] **D.4** Paginazione lista archiviate, indice `KanbanComment(cardId, createdAt)`, cap sul `take` dei reminder. **S**
- [ ] **D.5** Job Playwright in `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: la suite e2e kanban è l'unica copertura UI-level ed **è codice morto, non gira mai**. **M**

## 2. Quick win indipendenti (ognuna ≤ 1 riga, effetto utente immediato)

| Fix | File | Sintomo oggi |
|-----|------|--------------|
| `/kanban/${boardId}` → `/kanban?boardId=${boardId}` | `RemindersPage.tsx:129` | Ogni click su un reminder kanban scarica l'utente su `/notes` via il catch-all di `App.tsx:92` |
| `return` incondizionato quando `specificUserId` è settato | `notifications.ts:31` | Auto-assegnarsi una card manda "Alice ti ha assegnato X" a **tutti** gli altri membri |
| `orderBy desc` + `.reverse()` | `comments-chat.service.ts:24, 158` | Chat e commenti congelati sulle 50 righe più vecchie |
| `<KanbanBoardPage key={boardId} …>` | `KanbanPage.tsx:36` | Aprire la board B da una notifica mentre A è filtrata → B si apre col filtro attivo, che la rende silenziosamente read-only |
| `_count: {select: {cards: {where: {archivedAt: null}}}}` | `column.service.ts:73` | Una colonna Done auto-archiviata sembra vuota, abilita Delete, poi 400 e risorge — senza via d'uscita dalla UI |
| accettare e scrivere `position` | `routes/kanban.ts:416` + `column.service.ts:39` | **Il drag delle colonne non ha mai funzionato**: Zod droppa il campo, Prisma esegue un update vuoto, la coda cancella l'item come riuscito |
| `onBlur={handleAddCard}` | `KanbanColumn.tsx:296` | Il titolo digitato della nuova card viene buttato sul blur (`handleAddCard` già no-op su stringa vuota; l'input gemello a `:153` committa già sul blur) |
| wrap `onClose` con i due blur | `CardDetailModal.tsx` | Esc scarta una descrizione appena scritta, senza avviso |
| `broadcast(boardId, {type:'board:updated', boardId})` + import | `board.service.ts` `updateBoard` | Rename e delete board invisibili agli altri viewer (tipo evento e handler client **esistono già**) |

## 3. Rischi da non sottovalutare

| Fix proposta | Rischio | Mitigazione |
|---|---|---|
| Resequence di `moveCard` (2.2) | `KanbanCard.updatedAt` è `@updatedAt` e Prisma lo applica su `updateMany`; `archiveCompletedCards` filtra su `updatedAt <= cutoff` → un resequence naïf **azzera il timer di archiviazione a 7 giorni di tutta la colonna ad ogni drag** | Riscrivere solo le righe la cui posizione cambia davvero (diff prima della write), oppure spostare il predicato su un `completedAt` esplicito |
| Rimozione del pull shared (3.6) | È l'**unico** path che pruna le board shared revocate | Rilassare il filtro di ownership a `:318-321` **nello stesso commit**. E2E: `sharing.spec.ts` + `dexie.spec.ts` |
| Dirty guard su `column:deleted` / `card:deleted` | Crea zombie permanenti: il delete è autoritativo (cascade), e il prune di `syncPull` cancella solo righe `synced` | **Non farlo.** Fare invece la 3.3: far emergere il CREATE orfano come `failed` visibile |
| `@@unique([columnId, position])` | Insicuro: lo shift `increment: 1` lo viola transitoriamente **dentro** la transazione, e le board in produzione hanno già posizioni duplicate dal bug 2.2 → la migration fallirebbe sui dati reali. `schema.prisma` è TIER 1 | **Non aggiungerlo.** Il resequence + tiebreaker rendono l'ordine deterministico senza toccare il DB |
| Togliere il poll chat (5.3) prima della 0.4 | La chat muore **definitivamente** dopo qualsiasi risposta non-OK: il poll è l'unico path di update residuo | Dipendenza d'ordine dura, non una preferenza |
| `assigneeId: card.assigneeId ?? actorId` sul move | Regredisce comportamento documentato e testato (`card.service.ts:242`, asserito in `card.service.test.ts:508`) | Decisione di prodotto, non bugfix. Se si cambia, aggiornare il test **deliberatamente** |
| `if (existing.status === status)` in `respondToShareById` | Legalizza anche DECLINED → ACCEPTED: il destinatario può auto-ripristinare uno share rifiutato | Usare il predicato stretto: `existing.status !== 'PENDING' && !(existing.status === 'ACCEPTED' && status === 'DECLINED')` |
| `isDone: false` in `getUserKanbanReminders` | Rompe la pagina reminder: `RemindersPage.tsx:113` costruisce la sezione Done proprio da quelle righe | Aggiungere un `take` bounded, lasciare stare il predicato |
| `viewerId` sulle righe shared (3.1) | Fallisce chiuso: le righe pre-upgrade non hanno `viewerId` → le board shared spariscono finché il primo pull non le riscrive | Proprietà non indicizzata: **niente bump Dexie v16**. Nota in changelog |

## Appendice — tutti i finding verificati


### board-page-state

#### `high` · bug · M — Any board-query error evicts the user from the open board, silently
`frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx:65` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useKanbanBoard.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/kanbanService.ts`

- **Problema:** The redirect effect fires on `isError || !board` for every failure mode, not just "board is gone". Combined with `retry: false` in useKanbanBoard, one failed background refetch (429, 502, dropped Wi-Fi) throws the user out of the board they are working in, with no message and no way to retry.
- **Evidenza:** KanbanBoardPage.tsx:65-69 `useEffect(() => { if (!isLoading && (isError || !board)) navigate('/kanban', { replace: true }); }, [isLoading, isError, board, navigate])`. useKanbanBoard.ts:73 sets `retry: false`, so a single non-2xx flips isError permanently — and react-query keeps the previous `data`, so `board` is still present while isError is true: the effect still redirects. Every SSE event and every mutation calls `invalidateQueries` (useKanbanRealtime.ts:60, useKanbanMutations.ts:10-14), so background refetches are constant. Offline is the same trap by a different route: `getBoard` is network-only (kanbanService.ts:129-132, no Dexie fallback), default networkMode pauses the query, and `isLoading = isPending && isFetching` (verified in node_modules/@tanstack/query-core/build/modern/queryObserver.js:310) is false while paused, so `!board` → immediate redirect. An offline-first app with kanbanBoards/kanbanColumns/kanbanCards in Dexie cannot open a board offline at all.
- **Impatto utente:** A user editing a card description gets a rate-limited or 500 refetch; the page unmounts mid-typing (CardDetailModal saves on blur, so the edit is lost), the board list appears, and nothing explains why. Offline, tapping any board on the list bounces straight back to the list.
- **Fix:** Take `error` from useKanbanBoard and redirect only on a real gone-signal: `const s = (error as AxiosError)?.response?.status; if (s === 404 || s === 403) navigate('/kanban', {replace:true})`. For every other error render an inline error state with a Retry button (and keep rendering the last `board` when it exists). Optionally seed the query from Dexie so the board is readable offline.

#### `medium` · race · S — Every mutation refetches the server before its own sync push lands — optimistic moves visibly revert
`frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useKanbanMutations.ts:17` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useBoardDnD.ts`, `frontend/src/features/sync/syncService.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/kanbanService.ts`

- **Problema:** Kanban mutations write only to Dexie + syncQueue, so the mutation resolves before any server write. onSuccess then fires `flushSync()` (fire-and-forget syncPush) and `invalidateBoard()` in the same tick. The GET usually wins the race against the queued PUT, so the board query returns pre-mutation state and useBoardDnD overwrites the optimistic localColumns with it.
- **Evidenza:** useKanbanMutations.ts:17-19 `function flushSync() { syncPush().catch(() => {}); }` then e.g. moveCard onSuccess at :100-103 `flushSync(); invalidateBoard();`. mutationFn is Dexie-only (kanbanService.ts:372-386 writes db.kanbanCards + db.syncQueue and returns). useBoardDnD.ts:48-53 re-syncs `setLocalColumns(board.columns)` as soon as `isMoveInFlight` is false — and onSettled clears that flag right after onSuccess (react-query order: mutationOptions.onSuccess → mutateOptions.onSuccess → mutationOptions.onSettled → mutateOptions.onSettled), i.e. long before the server write. Worse, syncPush self-defers: syncService.ts:550-555 `if (isSyncing) { syncPushScheduled = true; return; }` with the retry scheduled 1s later (syncService.ts:816-818), so a second drag while a push is in flight delays the real PUT by ≥1s while the refetch happens immediately. Recovery depends entirely on the SSE card:moved echo (kanbanSSE.ts:102-113 broadcasts to all connections including the actor).
- **Impatto utente:** Drag two cards quickly: the second one snaps back to its old column for a second and then jumps again. If SSE is not connected, the card stays in the wrong column until the next manual refetch, and the user drags it a second time. Newly created cards have the same hole — createCard is Dexie-only (kanbanService.ts:284-346) but the board renders from the server query, so an added card is invisible until the POST lands.
- **Fix:** Make the invalidation follow the push instead of racing it: `function flushSync() { syncPush().catch(() => {}).finally(invalidateBoard); }` and delete the separate `invalidateBoard()` from the Dexie-backed mutations' onSuccess bodies (11 mechanical sites in this file).
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Real, but the headline impact is narrower than stated. broadcast() (kanbanSSE.ts:102-113) does echo card:moved to the actor's own connection, so a single move self-corrects after one extra round trip — a flash, not a stuck card. The multi-second wrong-column state the finding describes requires the syncPush self-defer path (a second mutation while a push is in flight) or a dead SSE stream (see the SSE finding). The proposed one-line fix (`syncPush().catch(()=>{}).finally(invalidateBoard)`) is still the right root fix.

#### `medium` · bug · M — Bulk move sends every card to the server twice and defeats its own grouped notification
`frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx:357` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useBoardDnD.ts`, `frontend/src/features/sync/syncService.ts`, `backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts`

- **Problema:** handleBulkMove calls `dnd.handleMoveCardToColumn` (which enqueues a real sync-queue move) AND then fires a direct `?silent=true` PUT for the same card. Each selected card is therefore moved twice on the server, and the queued copy is not silent, so it emits the per-card notification the grouped endpoint was added to replace.
- **Evidenza:** KanbanBoardPage.tsx:356-368: the first loop calls `dnd.handleMoveCardToColumn(...)` → useBoardDnD.ts:274-278 `mutations.moveCard.mutate(...)` → kanbanService.ts:372-386 enqueues a KANBAN_CARD UPDATE with columnId; syncService.ts:690-695 routes that to `PUT /kanban/cards/:id/move` with no `silent` flag. The second loop fires `api.put('/kanban/cards/:id/move?silent=true')` for the same card. Backend card.service.ts:272-290 sends `notifyBoardUsersTiered` whenever `skipNotification` is false, and logs a MOVED activity + reassigns the card at :242-252. Both writes also run the position-shifting transaction (card.service.ts:202-232) concurrently on the same rows. All failures are swallowed: `.catch(() => {})` on both the moves (line 364) and the grouped notify (line 368).
- **Impatto utente:** Marquee-select 8 cards and bulk-move them: every board member gets 8 individual 'moved card' notifications plus the grouped one, the activity log shows 16 MOVED entries, and if the silent PUTs 403/429 the cards stay moved on screen with no toast and no rollback.
- **Fix:** Add a persist flag to the optimistic mover — `handleMoveCardToColumn(cardId, targetColumnId, { persist = true } = {})` — and have handleBulkMove pass `{ persist: false }` so only the silent REST calls reach the server; replace the two `.catch(() => {})` with a toast + `invalidateBoard()` so a failed bulk move is visible.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** The notification/activity impact is misdiagnosed. `mutate()` is synchronous dispatch, so the silent PUTs in loop 2 go out before syncPush (which only starts from the mutation's async onSuccess). By the time the queued non-silent move arrives, card.columnId already equals toColumnId, so card.service.ts:243 (`if (actorId && card.columnId !== toColumnId)`) is false — no notifyBoardUsersTiered, no MOVED activity, no reassignment. So there are not 8 duplicate notifications or 16 activity rows in the normal path; what is real is a redundant second server write per card (extra rate-limit budget, extra position churn — relevant given the v1.10.3 spiral), a latent ordering hazard if the silent PUT is ever delayed or fails, and fully swallowed errors.

#### `medium` · bug · S — No key on KanbanBoardPage: switching boards keeps the previous board's state, including a filter that silently locks the new board read-only
`frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanPage.tsx:36` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useBoardDnD.ts`

- **Problema:** KanbanPage renders KanbanBoardPage at the same tree position with no `key`, so changing `?boardId=` swaps the prop without remounting. Every piece of page state survives: filters, selectedCardId, open modals, unread count, marquee selection, and useBoardDnD's localColumns.
- **Evidenza:** KanbanPage.tsx:34-37 `if (boardId) { return <KanbanBoardPage boardId={boardId} />; }` — no key. The surviving state is all plain useState in KanbanBoardPage.tsx:109-145 (filters at :118). `filtersActive` is fed into the columns as `readOnly={readOnly || filtersActive}` (lines 878 and 920), and KanbanColumn.tsx:182 hides the whole column menu when readOnly. useBoardDnD.ts:46-53 only re-syncs localColumns in an effect, so the first render after a boardId change paints the previous board's columns under the new board's header, and while `isMoveInFlight` is true it keeps painting them. Reachable paths: NotificationItem.tsx:43/52 and SharedWithMePage.tsx:459 link to /kanban?boardId=… while a board is already open, plus browser back/forward between two boardIds.
- **Impatto utente:** User filters board A by assignee, clicks a notification for board B: board B opens with the filter still on, most cards hidden, add-card and the column menus gone, and no obvious cause. They also see a flash of board A's cards under board B's title.
- **Fix:** `return <KanbanBoardPage key={boardId} boardId={boardId} />;` — one line, and it kills the whole class (filters, modals, unread badge, selection, stale localColumns).
- **Correzione del verificatore:** One sub-claim is weaker than stated: the 'flash of board A's cards under board B's title' only occurs when board B's query is already warm in cache (staleTime 5min). On a cold key, isLoading gates rendering with the spinner at KanbanBoardPage.tsx:373-379, so no stale columns are painted. The state-carryover half — filters silently locking the new board read-only — is fully confirmed, and the one-line `key={boardId}` fix is correct.

#### `medium` · perf · M — memo(KanbanCard) is defeated on every render; marquee drag re-renders all cards per mousemove
`frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx:873` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useBoardDnD.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useMarqueeSelection.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/components/KanbanColumn.tsx`

- **Problema:** KanbanCard is memoized, but two of its props change identity on every parent render: the inline `onCardSelect` arrow and `onMoveCardToColumn`, whose useCallback depends on the whole mutation object. So every KanbanBoardPage render re-renders every card — and marquee selection sets state on every mousemove.
- **Evidenza:** KanbanCard.tsx:36 `export default memo(function KanbanCard(...))`, receiving `onSelect={onCardSelect}` and `onMoveToColumn={onMoveCardToColumn}` straight through (KanbanColumn.tsx:257-271, and KanbanColumn itself is not memoized: KanbanColumn.tsx:30 `export default function`). KanbanBoardPage.tsx:873 and :915 pass `onCardSelect={(cardId) => setSelectedCardId(cardId)}` — a new closure each render. useBoardDnD.ts:245-281 memoizes handleMoveCardToColumn on `[mutations.moveCard]`, and useMutation returns a fresh object every render (node_modules/@tanstack/react-query/build/modern/useMutation.js:40 `return { ...result, mutate, mutateAsync }`), so the dep always changes — same defect in KanbanBoardPage.tsx:315-337 where six useCallbacks list `[mutations]`. useMarqueeSelection.ts:126-145 calls setMarqueeRect + setSelectedCardIds on every mousemove, and getIntersectedCardIds does a querySelectorAll + getBoundingClientRect per card (:61-72) in the same handler.
- **Impatto utente:** On a board with a few hundred cards, marquee-dragging a selection stutters: each pointer move forces a full layout read of every card plus a re-render of every card, even the ones whose selected state did not change.
- **Fix:** Make the identities stable: pass `onCardSelect={setSelectedCardId}` at both call sites, depend on the stable `.mutate` functions instead of the mutation objects (`[mutations.moveCard.mutate]`, `[mutations.updateCard.mutate]`, …), and wrap handleRenameColumn/handleDeleteColumn/handleAddCard/handleToggleColumnCompletion in useCallback. Then `isSelected` is the only prop that flips and memo does its job.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Accurate as a structural defect; the perf pain is only measurable on large boards (the finding already scopes it to 'a few hundred cards'), so this is fragility/wasted memoization rather than a bug users hit on a typical board.

#### `medium` · bug · S — SSE stream gives up permanently on any non-OK response — realtime and presence die silently
`frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useKanbanRealtime.ts:91` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx`

- **Problema:** The connect() routine schedules a 5s reconnect only from the catch block. A response that arrives with a non-2xx status returns early instead, leaving no reconnect timer, so the board loses realtime updates and presence for the rest of the session.
- **Evidenza:** useKanbanRealtime.ts:91 `if (!response.ok || !response.body) return;` versus :116-119 `catch { ... reconnectTimeout = setTimeout(connect, 5000); }`. The effect deps are `[boardId, handleEvent]` and both are stable, so nothing re-runs connect(). A 429 or 502 on `/api/kanban/boards/:id/events` therefore ends realtime silently — this repo already shipped a rate-limit spiral (v1.10.3), so 429 on that endpoint is not hypothetical.
- **Impatto utente:** After one transient error the presence avatars vanish and never come back, cards moved by teammates never appear, and — because the optimistic-move revert in finding 2 relies on the SSE echo to re-correct itself — a dragged card can stay visibly in the wrong column.
- **Fix:** Delete the early return and let the catch handle it: `if (!response.ok || !response.body) throw new Error('sse ' + response.status);` so the existing 5s reconnect path runs.

#### `medium` · a11y · S — Icon-only header buttons have no accessible name and the board menu has no Escape/focus handling
`frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx:444` · anche: `frontend/CLAUDE.md`, `frontend/src/locales/en.json`, `frontend/src/locales/it.json`

- **Problema:** The 1168-line page contains zero aria-labels. The back button and the three-dot board menu button are icon-only with neither text, title nor aria-label, and the menu itself is a plain div dismissed only by a click-catcher — no Escape, no aria-expanded, no focus return.
- **Evidenza:** `grep -n 'aria-label\|aria-expanded' KanbanBoardPage.tsx` returns nothing across 26 `<button>` elements. Back button KanbanBoardPage.tsx:444-449 renders only `<ArrowLeft size={20} />`; the menu trigger at :613-617 renders only `<MoreVertical size={18} />`; the menu at :619-624 is dismissed solely by `<div className="fixed inset-0 z-10" onClick={...} />`. frontend/CLAUDE.md mandates 'aria-label obbligatorio su tutti i bottoni icon-only' and focus trap + role=dialog on overlays. For contrast, useMarqueeSelection.ts:181-193 does register an Escape handler, so the inconsistency is within the same feature.
- **Impatto utente:** A screen-reader user hears 'button, button' in the board header with no way to know one goes back and one opens board actions; a keyboard user who opens the board menu cannot close it with Escape and must mouse-click the invisible backdrop.
- **Fix:** Add `aria-label={t('common.back')}` / `aria-label={t('common.moreActions')}` (plus `aria-haspopup="menu"` and `aria-expanded={modals.showBoardMenu}`) on the two buttons, and one Escape listener effect gated on `modals.showBoardMenu` that calls `setShowBoardMenu(false)`.

#### `low` · ux · S — Open card modal vanishes mid-edit when the card disappears from the board query
`frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx:1090` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/components/CardDetailModal.tsx`, `frontend/src/locales/en.json`, `frontend/src/locales/it.json`

- **Problema:** selectedCardId is never reconciled with board data. The modal's open state comes from the id while its content comes from a lookup that can go null (card deleted, archived, or moved by a teammate), and CardDetailModal renders nothing in that case — the modal just disappears with no explanation and the id stays set.
- **Evidenza:** KanbanBoardPage.tsx:1088-1096 `isOpen={!!selectedCardId}` with `card={selectedCard}`; selectedCard is the memo at :148-155 that searches board.columns and returns null when the card is absent. CardDetailModal.tsx:108 `if (!card) return null;`. An SSE card:deleted invalidates the board query (useKanbanRealtime.ts:60) so the refetch drops the card while the modal is open; the lazy archive in backend board.service.ts:143-144 can also remove a card on an ordinary GET.
- **Impatto utente:** A user is typing a description; a teammate deletes or archives the card, or lazy-archive sweeps it; the modal blinks out of existence and the typed text is gone with no message. Same silent close if the card is moved into a column filtered out by the active filter.
- **Fix:** Add one reconciling effect: `useEffect(() => { if (selectedCardId && board && !selectedCard) { setSelectedCardId(null); toast(t('kanban.card.removed')); } }, [selectedCardId, board, selectedCard])`, with the new key added to both en.json and it.json.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Two of the three cited triggers are wrong. (a) The filter path does not apply: selectedCard iterates `board.columns` (KanbanBoardPage.tsx:150), not the filtered displayColumns, so an active filter never hides the open card. (b) Lazy archive cannot hit an actively edited card: archiveCompletedCards (card.service.ts:374-401) only archives rows with `updatedAt <= cutoffDate` (ARCHIVE_AFTER_DAYS), and a card being edited has a fresh updatedAt. Also, the modal component stays mounted rendering null, so editDescription is not destroyed — it is just unreachable. The only real trigger is a teammate deleting the card you have open, which makes this narrow polish rather than a bug users hit in normal use.

#### `low` · dataloss · S — Column delete wipes the column's cards from Dexie and its only server-error toast can never fire
`frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx:205` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/kanbanService.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/components/KanbanColumn.tsx`, `backend/src/services/kanban/column.service.ts`

- **Problema:** handleDeleteColumn attaches an onError toast for the server's 'column has cards' rejection, but the mutation resolves as soon as the Dexie write succeeds, so onError never sees a server error. Meanwhile the local delete removes every card in the column from Dexie while the server keeps them.
- **Evidenza:** KanbanBoardPage.tsx:203-207 `mutations.deleteColumn.mutate(columnId, { onError: () => toast.error(t('kanban.column.hasCards')) })`. The mutationFn is Dexie-only: kanbanService.ts:252-280 does `db.kanbanCards.where('columnId').equals(columnId).delete()` then `db.kanbanColumns.delete()` and enqueues only a KANBAN_COLUMN DELETE — it never throws for a non-empty column. The backend does reject it: column.service.ts:76 `if (column._count.cards > 0) throw new BadRequestError('errors.kanban.columnHasCards')`, and since v1.10.4 a 400 marks the queue item `failed` immediately (syncService.ts:785-795) with no toast on this path. The UI guard at KanbanColumn.tsx:223-225 uses the rendered card count, so the delete is reachable whenever a card lands in the column between render and click (a teammate's card:created, or a card dragged in by another client).
- **Impatto utente:** Rare but real: the column and its cards vanish locally, the sync queue quietly holds a failed item, and the next board refetch resurrects everything — the user sees a delete that undid itself and, until then, cards missing from their offline cache.
- **Fix:** Mirror the server rule where the mutation can still fail: at the top of kanbanService.deleteColumn, `if (await db.kanbanCards.where('columnId').equals(columnId).count() > 0) throw new Error('kanban.column.hasCards');`. The existing onError toast then becomes reachable and the local cards are never deleted.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** 'dataloss' overstates it. Nothing is actually lost: the server still holds the cards and useKanbanBoard's hydration re-puts any card missing locally (useKanbanBoard.ts:52-57), and the failed queue item is not silent — SyncStatusIndicator.tsx:27 counts `status === 'failed'` and surfaces it with a retry action. The genuine defects are the unreachable onError toast (dead code where the author expected a server error) and a transient local-cache divergence, reachable only when a card lands in the column between render and click. The one-line mirror-the-rule fix in kanbanService.deleteColumn is still the right call.

### card-service

#### `high` · bug · M — moveCard: same-column downward reorder produces DUPLICATE positions + a hole
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:202` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useBoardDnD.ts`, `backend/src/services/kanban/board.service.ts`

- **Problema:** The three-step shift algorithm (increment target >= newPosition, set card, decrement source > oldPosition) is only correct when the card moves UP. On a downward same-column move the moved card is not yet removed from the list when the shift is computed, so the final decrement pass also decrements the card that already sits at newPosition. Two cards end up with the identical position and one slot is left empty.
- **Evidenza:** Lines 204-207 `updateMany({ where: { columnId: toColumnId, position: { gte: newPosition } }, data: { position: { increment: 1 } } })`, line 210-213 sets the card to `newPosition`, lines 216-224 `updateMany({ where: { columnId, position: { gt: card.position }, id: { not: cardId } }, data: { position: { decrement: 1 } } })` using the PRE-move `card.position` captured at line 187. I re-implemented these exact three statements and ran them: start `X@0 A@1 B@2 C@3 D@4`, move X to index 3 -> result `A@0 B@1 X@3 C@3 D@4` (expected `A@0 B@1 C@2 X@3 D@4`). Even a single-slot drag `A` 1->2 gives `X@0 A@2 B@2 C@3 D@4`. Upward moves are correct. The frontend sends the post-move array index: useBoardDnD.ts:241-244 does `arrayMove(sortedCards, oldIdx, newIdx)` then `mutate({ position: newIdx })`. board.service.ts:172 reads cards back with `orderBy: { position: 'asc' }` and NO tiebreaker, so a tie is resolved arbitrarily by Postgres.
- **Impatto utente:** Dragging any card DOWN inside a column (the single most common kanban gesture) leaves two cards sharing a position. The optimistic UI shows the right order, then the refetch triggered by useKanbanMutations `invalidateBoard()` re-sorts on the ambiguous positions and the card can visibly snap back or swap with its neighbour. Every later move/delete in that column compounds the corruption because the arithmetic assumes contiguous unique positions.
- **Fix:** Delete the three incremental updateMany calls and resequence instead: inside the existing $transaction, load `kanbanCard.findMany({ where: { columnId: <col>, archivedAt: null }, select: { id: true }, orderBy: { position: 'asc' } })` for the source and target column, splice the moved id into the target at `newPosition`, and write back `position: index` for each. ~15 lines, kills duplicates, holes, the off-by-one and the archived-gap bug (see separate finding) in one shot. Add `orderBy: [{ position: 'asc' }, { createdAt: 'asc' }]` at board.service.ts:172 so any legacy tie is at least deterministic.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Accurate on the mechanism, overstated on severity: this corrupts ordering, not data — no card is lost or made inaccessible, and positions still stay within the column. Per the calibration (critical = data loss or permission hole) this is high: a bug users hit on the most common gesture.

#### `high` · permission · S — moveCard never checks that toColumnId belongs to the card's board (cross-board card injection)
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:194` · anche: `backend/src/routes/kanban.ts`, `backend/src/services/kanbanPermissions.ts`

- **Problema:** `targetColumn` is fetched with its `boardId` selected, but that boardId is never compared to the card's board. The route only authorises the SOURCE card's board. So any user with WRITE on board A can move a card into any column of any board B in the system, given only that column's UUID.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:194-198 selects `targetColumn.boardId` and never uses it; line 200 `const boardId = card.column.boardId` (source board) is what everything downstream uses. routes/kanban.ts:459-465: `await getCardWithAccess(id, request.user.id, 'WRITE')` then `moveCardSchema.parse` where `toColumnId: z.string()` (not even `.uuid()`). kanbanPermissions.ts:41-53 `getCardWithAccess` resolves the board from the CARD only — nothing validates the destination.
- **Impatto utente:** A collaborator who was removed from a shared board still has that board's column UUIDs cached in Dexie (db.kanbanColumns), and can POST a card into it forever. In the other direction the card silently disappears from board A (getBoard filters by column->board) with no delete event, and board B's connected clients get no SSE at all because `broadcast()` on line 234 targets the source boardId — the card just materialises on their next refetch.
- **Fix:** One line after line 198: `if (targetColumn.boardId !== card.column.boardId) throw new BadRequestError('errors.kanban.columnNotFound');` — reuses an existing i18n key present in both en.json and it.json, so no new translations needed.

#### `high` · permission · S — updateCard lets any board writer set noteId directly, bypassing the owner check and creating an unremovable link
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:70` · anche: `backend/src/routes/kanban.ts`, `backend/src/services/kanban/linking.service.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useKanbanMutations.ts`

- **Problema:** `PUT /api/kanban/cards/:id` accepts `noteId` and writes it straight through, bypassing every guard in the dedicated link route: the note-owner check, the already-linked check, the `noteLinkedById` bookkeeping and the NOTE_LINKED activity. Because `noteLinkedById` stays null, the unlink route then refuses everybody.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:55 (`noteId?: string | null` in the signature) and line 70 `if (data.noteId !== undefined) updateData.noteId = data.noteId;`, exposed by routes/kanban.ts:53 `noteId: z.string().nullable().optional()`. Compare linking.service.ts:80 `if (card.noteId) throw new BadRequestError('cardAlreadyLinkedNote')`, :89 `if (note.userId !== actorId) throw new ForbiddenError('errors.kanban.onlyOwnerCanLink')`, :93 `data: { noteId, noteLinkedById: actorId }`. Then linking.service.ts:144 `if (card.noteLinkedById !== actorId) throw new ForbiddenError('errors.kanban.onlyLinkerCanUnlinkNote')` — with `noteLinkedById === null` that is true for every user, so the link is permanent. The PUT also returns the card through `cardWithAssigneeSelect` (helpers.ts:46 includes `note: { id, title, userId }`) with no visibility filtering, unlike getBoard which nulls inaccessible notes (board.service.ts:224-232), so the response echoes the title of a note the caller may not own. The frontend never sends noteId through this mutation (useKanbanMutations.ts:88-98 lists title/description/assigneeId/dueDate/priority only) — it always uses linkNoteToCard.
- **Impatto utente:** A shared-board collaborator can bind an arbitrary note UUID to a card, read back its title in the HTTP response, and leave the card permanently linked to a note nobody — not even the note's owner — can detach through the UI. It also silently overwrites an existing link while leaving `noteLinkedById` pointing at the previous linker, handing unlink rights to an unrelated user.
- **Fix:** Delete the field from both places: drop `noteId` from `updateCardSchema` (routes/kanban.ts:53) and from `updateCard`'s param type + line 70. Zero frontend impact — linking already has its own authorised route.

#### `medium` · bug · M — Archived cards keep their positions, so every drop index in that column lands in the wrong slot
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:387` · anche: `backend/src/services/kanban/board.service.ts`

- **Problema:** `archiveCompletedCards` only stamps `archivedAt` — it never resequences the column. Archived cards therefore keep occupying positions 0..n. But the board API hides them, so the frontend's drop index is an index into the VISIBLE list, while moveCard's `gte`/`gt` arithmetic runs over ALL cards including archived ones. The two coordinate systems don't match.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:387-394 `updateMany({ ... data: { archivedAt: new Date() } })` with no position rewrite. board.service.ts:170-172 `cards: { where: { archivedAt: null }, orderBy: { position: 'asc' } }`. moveCard's three updateMany calls (204, 217, 227) have no `archivedAt` filter. Simulated: column `arch@0 arch@1 V1@2 V2@3 V3@4`, user drags V3 between V1 and V2 (visible index 1) -> visible order becomes `V3, V1, V2` instead of `V1, V3, V2`. Cross-column: `done` column holding arch@0-2 and V1@3, dropping a new card at visible index 1 (below V1) puts it at position 1 -> visible order `N, V1` instead of `V1, N`.
- **Impatto utente:** Auto-archive runs on every single board GET (board.service.ts:144) for the completed column, so within a week every board has archived cards sitting in its Done column's position space. From then on, dropping a card at a chosen slot in that column puts it somewhere else, and the user has to drag repeatedly. It looks exactly like the drag-and-drop being broken.
- **Fix:** Scope the ordering to live cards only. If you take the resequence fix above, add `archivedAt: null` to the findMany that rebuilds the order and the whole class disappears. Minimal standalone patch: add `archivedAt: null` to the where clauses on lines 205, 219 and 228, and set `position` to `-1` (or resequence the column) when archiving at line 393 so archived rows leave the position space entirely.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Real, but narrower than stated. It affects only columns that actually contain archived cards (i.e. isCompleted columns, after 7 days of inactivity), and it does NOT create duplicate positions — the `gte` increment keeps positions unique; the card simply lands earlier than intended by the number of archived rows sitting below the target slot. Degraded ordering in the Done column, not corruption.

#### `medium` · dataloss · S — moveCard silently reassigns the card to whoever dragged it, outside the transaction and with no activity entry
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:243` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/KanbanBoardPage.tsx`

- **Problema:** Every cross-column move overwrites `assigneeId` with the mover's id, clobbering a deliberate assignment. Unlike updateCard, this write logs no ASSIGNED activity and notifies neither the old nor the new assignee, so the change leaves no trace anywhere. It also runs after the $transaction has committed, together with the TaskItem sync and the reminder completion.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:243-247 `if (actorId && card.columnId !== toColumnId) { await prisma.kanbanCard.update({ where: { id: cardId }, data: { assigneeId: actorId } }); }` — no read of the current assignee, no guard. The MOVED activity logged right after (249-252) records only the column change. Compare updateCard:88-97 which does log ASSIGNED/UNASSIGNED. The transaction ends at line 232, so this update, the TaskItem sync (255-270) and the reminder auto-complete (316-321) are four separate non-atomic writes. The marquee bulk move hits the same path: KanbanBoardPage.tsx:360-365 fires one `PUT /cards/:id/move?silent=true` per selected card and `silent` only suppresses the notification (line 273), not the reassignment.
- **Impatto utente:** A project lead assigns 20 cards; a teammate marquee-selects them and drags them to In Progress, and all 20 are silently reassigned to the teammate with nothing in any card's activity feed to explain it. The original assignees just stop seeing their work.
- **Fix:** Fetch `assigneeId` in the existing card lookup at line 184-190 and only auto-assign when it is null: `data: { assigneeId: card.assigneeId ?? actorId }` — or, if reassignment is genuinely wanted, move the write inside the $transaction and emit `logCardActivity(cardId, actorId, 'ASSIGNED', ...)` when it actually changes.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Not accidental data loss — it is deliberate, documented and tested behaviour (comment at card.service.ts:242 'auto-assign card to the mover', plus a dedicated test 'auto-assigns card to mover on cross-column move' at card.service.test.ts:508-533). The defensible defect is narrower: it overwrites an EXPLICIT assignee rather than only filling a null one, and it leaves no ASSIGNED activity or notification, so a bulk move silently reassigns a whole selection. The `assigneeId: card.assigneeId ?? actorId` fix is right, but it changes intended product behaviour and should be confirmed, not slipped in.

#### `medium` · test · S — The card position algorithm has zero behavioural test coverage — updateMany is mocked out
`backend/src/services/kanban/__tests__/card.service.test.ts:430` · anche: `backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts`

- **Problema:** moveCard has seven tests and none of them exercise the ordering maths: `prismaMock.kanbanCard.updateMany` is stubbed to `{ count: 0 }` and the assertions check that `$transaction` was called and that a broadcast fired. Same-column reordering — the case that is actually broken — is not tested at all.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.test.ts:444-445 `prismaMock.kanbanCard.updateMany.mockResolvedValue({ count: 0 }); prismaMock.kanbanCard.update.mockResolvedValue({});` then 450-461 `expect(prismaMock.$transaction).toHaveBeenCalled()` and an `expect(broadcast).toHaveBeenCalledWith(...)`. Every moveCard case in the file (430, 463, 489, 495, 508, 535, 564) passes `targetColumn.id !== sourceColumn.id`; there is no test where toColumnId equals the card's current column. deleteCard's reposition test (608) is mocked the same way.
- **Impatto utente:** The most-used interaction on the feature (drag a card) can regress or stay broken indefinitely while the suite stays green — which is exactly what happened with the downward-move duplicate positions.
- **Fix:** Extract the ordering into a pure function, e.g. `computeColumnOrder(cardIds: string[], cardId: string, newIndex: number): string[]`, call it from moveCard, and table-test it with no Prisma mock at all: up, down, adjacent, first, last, cross-column. Ten lines of test that would have caught this.

#### `low` · race · S — createCard: read-then-write on max(position) with no transaction, no constraint, no tiebreaker
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:23` · anche: `backend/src/services/kanban/board.service.ts`, `backend/prisma/schema.prisma`

- **Problema:** The next position is computed with a separate `aggregate` and used in a later `create`, outside any transaction. Two concurrent creates in the same column both read the same max and both write max+1. Nothing in the system ever detects or repairs a duplicate position.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:23-27 `const maxPos = await prisma.kanbanCard.aggregate({ where: { columnId }, _max: { position: true } }); const position = (maxPos._max.position ?? -1) + 1;` then a separate `create` at 29-32. schema.prisma KanbanCard declares only `@@index([columnId])` — there is no `@@unique([columnId, position])`. board.service.ts:172 orders cards by `position` alone, so a tie has no deterministic resolution.
- **Impatto utente:** Two people adding a card to the same column at the same moment (or a card added by the note->kanban conversion while a collaborator types one in) get two cards at the same position; their relative order then flips between page loads for everyone on the board.
- **Fix:** Don't add a unique constraint (the move code's `position = position + 1` updateMany would transiently violate it). Cheapest real fix: make the ordering deterministic — `orderBy: [{ position: 'asc' }, { createdAt: 'asc' }]` at board.service.ts:172 — and wrap lines 23-32 in `prisma.$transaction` so at least the read and write are one round trip.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Real but the narrowest of the position problems: it needs two creates in the same column interleaving between the aggregate and the create. In practice the duplicate positions users actually see come from the moveCard downward-reorder bug and from the marquee bulk move, which hardcodes `position: 999` for every selected card (KanbanBoardPage.tsx:361-364) — that one plants identical positions deterministically, not by race.

#### `low` · architecture · S — deleteCard leaves no audit trail at all: dead actorId param, dead selects, DELETED activity never emitted
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:325` · anche: `backend/src/routes/kanban.ts`, `backend/src/services/kanban/helpers.ts`

- **Problema:** On a shared board a card can be deleted with no record of who did it and no notification to anyone not currently watching the board. The half-finished intent is visible in the code: the function takes an `actorId` the route never passes, selects `title` and `column.title` it never uses, and the `DELETED` action exists in the enum, the helper union, the frontend type and the i18n catalogue but is never written by any code path.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:325 `export async function deleteCard(cardId: string, actorId?: string)` — `actorId` is referenced nowhere in the body (325-350). Line 328 selects `title: true` and `column: { select: { boardId: true, title: true } }` but only `columnId`, `position` and `column.boardId` are used. routes/kanban.ts:485-489 calls `kanbanService.deleteCard(id)` without the actor. `grep -rn "'DELETED'"` over backend/src returns exactly one hit: the union in helpers.ts:10. The frontend already renders it (CardDetailModal.tsx:247-248 `case 'DELETED': return t('kanban.activity.DELETED')`) and schema.prisma has `DELETED` in `KanbanCardAction`.
- **Impatto utente:** A card disappears from a shared board and nobody who was not connected via SSE at that instant ever learns it existed. Compare a move, which sends a tiered notification plus email. There is no way to answer "who deleted my card".
- **Fix:** Smallest useful change: pass the actor from routes/kanban.ts:487 and, before the delete, reuse the existing `notifyBoardUsersTiered(actorId, boardId, 'KANBAN_CARD_MOVED', ...)` pattern with the already-selected `card.title` — the data it needs is being fetched and thrown away. If no trail is wanted, delete the dead param and the two unused selects so the next reader isn't misled.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** 'Half-finished intent' is wrong: card.service.ts:334-337 is an explicit comment documenting the decision NOT to log DELETED, and the reasoning is sound — KanbanCardActivity cascades with the card, so a card-scoped DELETED row could never be read back. What actually stands is dead code (the unused actorId param and two unused selects) plus the absence of a delete notification. Fixing it properly needs a board-level log, which the comment already scopes out; the lazy move is to delete the dead param and selects.

#### `low` · bug · S — updateCard logs DUE_DATE_SET and description-UPDATED without comparing the old value, and resets every user's reminder
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:98` · anche: `backend/src/services/kanbanReminder.service.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/components/CardDetailModal.tsx`

- **Problema:** The DUE_DATE_SET branch and the description branch fire on every request that carries the field, with no comparison against the current value — `currentCard.dueDate` is fetched but only consulted for the removal case, and `description` isn't even selected. Each no-op save appends an activity row and, for dueDate, rewrites every board member's reminder with `isDone: false`.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:98-102 `if (data.dueDate !== undefined) { if (data.dueDate !== null) { await logCardActivity(cardId, actorId, 'DUE_DATE_SET', ...) } ... }` — no `!== currentCard.dueDate`, unlike the title branch at line 125 which does compare. Lines 130-134 `if (data.description !== undefined) await logCardActivity(cardId, actorId, 'UPDATED', { metadata: { field: 'description' } })` — `description` is absent from the select at line 62. Line 116-118 routes an unchanged date to `updateRemindersForCard`, which is kanbanReminder.service.ts:57-60 `updateMany({ where: { cardId }, data: { dueDate: newDueDate, isDone: false } })` for ALL users. The trigger is a native date input: CardDetailModal.tsx:131-137 `handleDueDateChange` mutates on every onChange, including arrow-key stepping through the year.
- **Impatto utente:** Stepping a due date with the keyboard writes one activity row per intermediate value; the activity feed is paginated at 50 (routes/kanban.ts, paginationSchema) so genuine history is pushed off the first page. Worse, every one of those writes flips `isDone` back to false on the reminders of every board member, so dismissed reminders keep resurrecting.
- **Fix:** Compare before acting, the way the title branch already does: `const newDue = data.dueDate ? new Date(data.dueDate).getTime() : null; const oldDue = currentCard.dueDate?.getTime() ?? null; if (newDue !== oldDue) { ...log + reminder work... }`. Add `description: true` to the select at line 62 and guard line 130 with `data.description !== currentCard.description`.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** The 'no-op save' framing is not reachable from the app. The description path is already guarded client-side (CardDetailModal.tsx:127-129 compares before mutating) and a date input only fires change when the value actually changes, so the unguarded backend branches are only reachable by a direct API call or by stepping the date with arrow keys. Real residue: one activity row plus one global `isDone: false` reminder reset per intermediate date value while stepping, and a backend that would happily log an unchanged description. Polish, not a bug users report.

#### `low` · ux · S — Bulk archive and lazy archive mutate the board without any SSE broadcast
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:486` · anche: `backend/src/services/kanbanSSE.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useKanbanRealtime.ts`

- **Problema:** `executeBulkArchive` and `archiveCompletedCards` remove cards from every collaborator's view but emit no realtime event, while every other card mutation in this file broadcasts. Connected clients keep rendering cards that the server considers archived.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:486-499 (`updateMany` + `logger.info`, no `broadcast`) and 387-400 (same). Every sibling operation does broadcast: createCard:34, updateCard:85, moveCard:234, deleteCard:349, unarchiveCard:441. The frontend only refetches the board on an SSE event (useKanbanRealtime.ts:61 `queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.kanban.board(boardId) })`), so nothing invalidates.
- **Impatto utente:** The owner runs Bulk Archive on 50 done cards; every teammate with the board open keeps seeing all 50, can open and drag them, and their moves hit archived cards on the server. The stale view persists until they navigate away and back.
- **Fix:** One line at the end of both functions, guarded by `result.count > 0`: `broadcast(boardId, { type: 'board:updated', boardId })`. That variant already exists in kanbanSSE.ts:41 and the frontend's generic else-branch invalidates the board query for any unrecognised event.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** 'The frontend only refetches the board on an SSE event' is false — the board is a normal react-query query (useKanbanBoard.ts) under the app defaults in lib/queryClient.ts (staleTime 5 min, refetchOnWindowFocus left at its default true), so a teammate's stale view self-corrects on window focus or remount. The lazy-archive half is close to harmless: archiveCompletedCards runs inside every getBoard (board.service.ts:144) and the same response already excludes the rows it just archived. Bounded staleness, not a persistent divergence.

#### `low` · perf · S — getArchivedCards returns the entire archive with no limit
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:406` · anche: `backend/src/routes/kanban.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/components/ArchivedCardsModal.tsx`

- **Problema:** The archived-cards endpoint has no take/skip and no pagination, while the archive is designed to grow forever (every completed card is auto-archived after 7 days). Each row carries assignee, note, column and a comment count subquery.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:406-423 `findMany({ where: { column: { boardId }, archivedAt: { not: null } }, orderBy: { archivedAt: 'desc' }, select: { ...cardWithAssigneeSelect, column: ... } })` — no `take`. `cardWithAssigneeSelect` (helpers.ts:30-48) includes `_count: { select: { comments: true } }`. routes/kanban.ts:566-570 passes no pagination even though the file defines a reusable `paginationSchema` (routes/kanban.ts:69-72) used by the activities endpoint. ArchivedCardsModal.tsx:44 maps and renders every row.
- **Impatto utente:** On a board that has been in use for a year the Archive modal downloads and renders thousands of cards in one shot — a multi-second freeze on mobile, and a payload that dwarfs the board itself.
- **Fix:** Reuse the existing schema: parse `paginationSchema` in the route and pass `skip`/`take` through, exactly as `GET /cards/:id/activities` already does. Or, if you don't want a paging UI yet, a bare `take: 200` plus a 'showing most recent 200' hint.

#### `low` · architecture · S — card:unarchived is broadcast by the backend but missing from the frontend SSE event union
`backend/src/services/kanban/card.service.ts:441` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/types.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useKanbanRealtime.ts`, `backend/src/services/kanbanSSE.ts`

- **Problema:** The SSE event contract is declared twice — once in the backend and once in the frontend — and the two copies have drifted. `unarchiveCard` emits an event type the frontend's union does not contain, so it type-checks only because the payload is cast from JSON.parse.
- **Evidenza:** card.service.ts:441-445 `broadcast(card.column.boardId, { type: 'card:unarchived', ... })`. Backend union kanbanSSE.ts:28-42 has both `card:unarchived` (line 33) and `board:updated` (line 41). Frontend union types.ts:170-183 has neither. useKanbanRealtime.ts:106 does `const event: KanbanSSEEvent = JSON.parse(line.slice(6))`, so the mismatch is invisible to TypeScript, and `updateDexieFromSSE`'s switch has no case for it — the Dexie cache silently keeps the card marked archived.
- **Impatto utente:** Today it degrades gracefully (the generic else-branch still invalidates the board query), but the offline Dexie copy is left stale, and any future `switch` narrowed on the frontend union will silently drop these events. The next event type added on one side and not the other fails the same silent way.
- **Fix:** Add the two missing variants to frontend/src/features/kanban/types.ts:170-183 and a `case 'card:unarchived'` in `updateDexieFromSSE` that clears the local archived flag. Longer term the union belongs in one shared file imported by both sides rather than being retyped.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** The stated user impact is wrong. LocalKanbanCard (lib/db.ts:131-149) has no `archivedAt` field at all — archived cards are simply absent from the board payload and are pruned from Dexie on the next pull (syncService.ts:404-411) — so nothing can be 'left marked archived' locally, and the invalidation in the else-branch plus useKanbanBoard's hydration re-adds the card. Also note the drift runs both ways: `{ type: 'connected' }` exists in the frontend union but not in the backend's KanbanEvent. This is type-contract hygiene, not a user-visible defect.

### column-board-service

#### `critical` · dataloss · M — createBoard mints its own column IDs for a client-supplied board ID, so cards created against local column IDs are silently dropped
`backend/src/services/kanban/board.service.ts:126` · anche: `backend/src/routes/kanban.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/kanbanService.ts`, `frontend/src/features/sync/syncService.ts`, `frontend/src/components/editor/TransformToKanbanModal.tsx`

- **Problema:** createBoard accepts a client-supplied board `id` but always generates fresh server UUIDs for the 3 default columns. The frontend therefore has to reconcile local↔server column IDs after the push, and that reconciliation rewrites Dexie rows but NOT the payloads already sitting in the sync queue.
- **Evidenza:** board.service.ts:120-137 `tx.kanbanBoard.create({ data: { ...(id ? { id } : {}), ..., columns: { create: [{title: cols.todo, position: 0}, ...] } } })` — the board honours the client id, the columns do not. frontend/src/features/kanban/kanbanService.ts:88-93 creates 3 Dexie columns with `uuidv4()` and queues them as `_localColumnIds`. syncService.ts:646-668 reconciles by position inside `db.transaction('rw', db.kanbanColumns, db.kanbanCards, ...)` — `db.syncQueue` is NOT in that transaction and is never rewritten. syncService.ts:686-688 pushes card creates to `/kanban/columns/${item.data.columnId}/cards`, i.e. the columnId snapshotted at enqueue time. routes/kanban.ts:446-449 → getColumnWithAccess → NotFoundError (404, utils/errors.ts:9). syncService.ts:780-784 deletes any queue item that gets 404/410, with only a console.warn.
- **Impatto utente:** In the note→Kanban wizard (TransformToKanbanModal.tsx:143-144 sets selectedBoardId then reads columns from Dexie at :46-52 — the v1.10.5 fix), a user creates a NEW board and immediately picks a column. If the board CREATE push hasn't round-tripped yet (offline, slow link, or the POST /boards 10/min rate limit backing off), every converted card is queued against a local column UUID, 404s, and is silently deleted from the queue. The cards stay in Dexie as 'created' forever so the user sees no error, but they never reach the server and vanish for every other device and collaborator.
- **Fix:** Let createBoard accept the client's column ids the same way it accepts the board id: add `columnIds?: [string, string, string]` to createBoardSchema (routes/kanban.ts:20-25) and spread `...(columnIds?.[i] ? { id: columnIds[i] } : {})` into each nested column create. Server ids then equal local ids and the entire reconciliation block in syncService.ts:646-668 can be deleted — a smaller net diff than hardening the remap.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Real, and the mechanism is exactly as described, with two qualifications. (1) The window is narrower than 'a user creates a NEW board and immediately picks a column': createBoard's mutation fires flushSync() synchronously (useKanbanMutations.ts:21-24), so on a healthy connection the board POST and the Dexie reconcile normally land before the user clicks a column and the card then carries a server id. The guaranteed-loss cases are offline (the flagship offline-first path), a stalled/failed board push, and the 500-on-retry of finding #4. (2) It is server-side loss, not local destruction — the cards stay readable in Dexie on that device forever; they simply never reach the server, other devices or collaborators, with a fully green sync indicator. Smaller root fix than changing the API contract: in syncService.ts:686-687 resolve the columnId from Dexie at push time (`(await db.kanbanCards.get(item.entityId))?.columnId ?? item.data.columnId`) — one line, and it also covers a card whose column changed after enqueue.

#### `high` · bug · S — Column reorder never persists: PUT /columns/:id strips `position`, so updateColumn issues an empty Prisma update
`backend/src/services/kanban/column.service.ts:37` · anche: `backend/src/routes/kanban.ts`, `frontend/src/features/kanban/kanbanService.ts`

- **Problema:** The frontend reorders columns by queueing one KANBAN_COLUMN UPDATE per column carrying only `{ position }`. The route's Zod schema has no `position` field, so Zod silently strips it and updateColumn builds an empty `updateData`. The dedicated reorderColumns endpoint that would do the job is never called by the app.
- **Evidenza:** column.service.ts:37-44 `const updateData = {}; if (data.title !== undefined) ...; if (data.isCompleted !== undefined) ...; prisma.kanbanColumn.update({ where, data: updateData })` — `position` is never assigned. routes/kanban.ts:417-420 `updateColumnSchema = z.object({ title?, isCompleted? })` (non-strict → unknown keys stripped). frontend kanbanService.ts:234-249 `reorderColumns` queues `data: { position }` per column; syncService.ts:680-681 sends it to `api.put('/kanban/columns/${id}', item.data)`. A grep of the whole repo for `columns/reorder` finds it only in backend routes/tests — no frontend caller exists (frontend/src/features/kanban/hooks/useBoardDnD.ts:208 calls the local Dexie helper, whose first arg is even named `_boardId`).
- **Impatto utente:** A user drags a column to a new place. It moves, the mutation reports success, no error appears. The server order is unchanged, so on the next syncPull (syncService.ts:383-391 bulkPuts the server columns over the now-'synced' local rows) or on any hard reload, the board snaps back to the old order. Reordering columns is simply impossible and fails silently, every time, for everyone.
- **Fix:** Two lines: add `position: z.number().int().min(0).optional()` to updateColumnSchema (routes/kanban.ts:417) and `if (data.position !== undefined) updateData.position = data.position;` in updateColumn (column.service.ts:39). That fixes the shared path every caller already routes through; the now-redundant reorderColumns service + endpoint can then be deleted.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Right about persistence, slightly wrong about the symptom: the drag doesn't even hold visually. useBoardDnD.ts:203-204 does `arrayMove(localColumns, ...)` without rewriting each column's `position` field, and the rendered list is `sortedColumns` = `[...localColumns].sort((a,b) => a.position - b.position)` (useBoardDnD.ts:303-306, consumed at KanbanBoardPage.tsx:173-178) — so the re-sort puts the columns straight back in the original order on drop. The proposed two-line backend fix is necessary but not sufficient: the optimistic state must also carry the new positions (`reordered.map((col,i) => ({...col, position: i}))`) or the drop still flickers back until the refetch lands.

#### `high` · bug · S — deleteColumn counts archived cards, so deleting a visually empty column 400s and the column resurrects
`backend/src/services/kanban/column.service.ts:73` · anche: `frontend/src/features/kanban/components/KanbanColumn.tsx`, `frontend/src/features/sync/syncService.ts`

- **Problema:** deleteColumn refuses when `_count.cards > 0`, counting ALL cards including archived ones. Every other read path hides archived cards, so the UI's own 'is this column empty' check disagrees with the server's.
- **Evidenza:** column.service.ts:71-76 `select: { boardId: true, _count: { select: { cards: true } } }` … `if (column._count.cards > 0) throw new BadRequestError('errors.kanban.columnHasCards')` — no `where: { archivedAt: null }`. board.service.ts:170-171 loads cards with `where: { archivedAt: null }`, and KanbanColumn.tsx:72 derives `hasCards = column.cards.length > 0` from that filtered payload, gating the delete button at :223-225. Archived cards are counted separately at board level only (board.service.ts:190-195). Cards land in the archive automatically after 7 days in the completed column (card.service.ts:368, 374-394).
- **Impatto utente:** A column whose cards were all archived (manually, via bulk archive, or by the 7-day lazy auto-archive) renders as empty, so the delete button is enabled. The frontend deletes the column and its cards from Dexie (kanbanService.ts:256-268) and queues the DELETE; the server answers 400, which syncPush treats as permanent (syncService.ts:785-795) — the item is marked 'failed', the sync-error banner appears, and the column reappears on the next pull. The user cannot delete it at all without first going into Archived cards and unarchiving/deleting each one.
- **Fix:** Count only live cards and let the schema cascade handle the rest: `_count: { select: { cards: { where: { archivedAt: null } } } }` on column.service.ts:73. KanbanCard.column is already `onDelete: Cascade` (schema.prisma:503), so `prisma.kanbanColumn.delete` removes the archived rows automatically.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Accurate; one narrowing worth stating. Archived cards can only be produced inside a column that is (or was) flagged isCompleted — the lazy archive filters `isCompleted: true` (card.service.ts:377-380) and both bulk-archive paths do too (card.service.ts:459-462, 486-491); there is no per-card archive action. So the trap is specifically the Done column (or a column later un-flagged via 'removeCompleted' while still holding archived cards) — which is exactly the column a user cleans up after the 7-day auto-archive, and it is unrecoverable from the board UI.

#### `medium` · dataloss · S — createBoard is not idempotent on a duplicate client id — P2002 becomes a 500 and poisons the sync queue
`backend/src/services/kanban/board.service.ts:119` · anche: `backend/src/services/note.service.ts`, `frontend/src/features/sync/syncService.ts`, `backend/src/app.ts`

- **Problema:** createBoard takes a client-supplied `id` but has no P2002 guard, unlike note.service which handles exactly this case. A retried board CREATE (the normal outcome of a lost response on a mobile connection) hits the PK unique constraint, and the global error handler maps only P2025, so the client sees a 500.
- **Evidenza:** board.service.ts:119-139 — bare `tx.kanbanBoard.create({ data: { ...(id ? { id } : {}) ... } })`, no try/catch. Contrast note.service.ts:70-76: `if (... code === 'P2002') { const existing = await prisma.note.findUnique({ where: { id } }); if (existing) return existing; }`. app.ts:88-92 maps P2025 → 404; P2002 falls through to the 500 fallback. syncService.ts:806-808 sends 500s to recordFailure → exponential backoff → 'failed' after MAX_RETRIES (syncService.ts:534-537).
- **Impatto utente:** User creates a board, the POST commits server-side but the response is lost (tunnel, tab suspend, flaky wifi). Every retry 500s until the item is marked 'failed': the user gets a permanent sync-error banner for a board that actually exists, and because the retry never returns a body, the column-ID reconciliation at syncService.ts:646-668 never runs — the local columns keep their local UUIDs and every subsequent card created in them 404s and is silently dropped.
- **Fix:** Copy the note.service pattern: wrap the create in try/catch and on `code === 'P2002'` return the existing board with the same `include: { columns: { orderBy: { position: 'asc' } } }`, so the retry is a no-op that still feeds the client the column list it needs.
- **Correzione del verificatore:** Real and correctly diagnosed, but it needs a response lost after a committed POST (tab suspend, tunnel, proxy timeout) — not something users hit on every session — so medium rather than high. The knock-on claim is accurate: a 500 retry never returns `res.data.columns`, so the reconcile at syncService.ts:646-668 never runs and finding #1's card 404s follow.

#### `medium` · permission · S — reorderColumns writes columns without checking they belong to the board the caller has access to
`backend/src/services/kanban/column.service.ts:60` · anche: `backend/src/routes/kanban.ts`

- **Problema:** The route authorises the board named in the URL, then reorderColumns updates whatever column IDs the body contains. `boardId` is used only for the SSE broadcast — it never constrains the writes.
